# Skills · S · part 98

852 entries, sennya to sentiment-analysis. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sennya**: Drive the sennya terminal from inside it. Open a side task in a new Claude Code session next to you, run sennya profiles, read or type into other terminals, arrange panes, resume past Claude Code chats, and edit sennya's config (profiles, shortcuts, themes). Use when $SENNYA_SESSION is set and the user wants work done in another session, terminal, pane, or tab; when they mention sennya; or when they ask to set up a sennya shortcut, profile, or setting. ([avfe/sennya](https://github.com/avfe/sennya/tree/HEAD/skills/sennya))
- **senolytic-car-t-metabolic**: Tier C index only (mouse study): do not use it to answer a person's own health question. Amor et al., Nature Aging 2024: in mice, uPAR-targeted senolytic CAR T cells (m.uPAR-m.28z) gave lasting protection against age-related metabolic dysfunction. Holds the paper's dual-control P-value rule. Use when someone asks about this study, senolytic CAR T cells and metabolism in mice, or uPAR; not for a person's own measurements. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/senolytic-car-t-metabolic) · ★1)
- **senolytic-human-brain-aging**: Tier C index only (cultured cells, mouse study): do not use it to answer a person's own health question. Aguado et al., Nature Aging 2023: in human brain organoids and mice, senolytics (navitoclax, ABT-737, dasatinib plus quercetin) reduced ageing markers and COVID-19 neuropathology. Holds the gene-ranking score. Use when someone asks about this study, senolytics in brain organoids or mice, or the listed compounds; not for a person's own measurements. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/senolytic-human-brain-aging) · ★1)

- **senpai**: Keeps the user learning while the agent writes code. Tags each idea in a change by the career level that must know it (junior, mid, senior, staff). Ideas at the user's level become a small "implement this" hole for them to fill; ideas above it get a short lesson; everything else the agent just writes. Level is saved and moves up as the user grows. Use when the user says "senpai", "teach me as you go", "I want to learn this", "don't let me get rusty", "senpai mid", or invokes /senpai. Off with "stop senpai". ([6igtree/senpai](https://github.com/6igtree/senpai/tree/HEAD/skills/senpai) · ★1)
